### Task 10: The History tab

**Files:**
- Create: `ui/src/admin/HistoryPane.svelte`, `ui/src/admin/HistoryPane.test.ts`
- Modify: `ui/src/lib/types.ts` (`SessionRow`, lines 21–40; new `SavedCounts`, `HistoryRow`, `HistoryPayload`)
- Modify: `ui/src/admin/router.svelte.ts` (whole file, 59 lines), `ui/src/admin/router.test.ts`
- Modify: `ui/src/admin/session.svelte.ts` (fields 48–63, `#showRow` 188–196, `dispose` 199–205; new `loadHistory`), `ui/src/admin/session.test.ts`
- Modify: `ui/src/admin/SessionDetail.svelte` (imports, props 16–23, totals 244–248, tabs 284–288, panes 300), `ui/src/admin/Admin.svelte` (line 101)
- Modify: `ui/src/admin/SiteDataPane.svelte` (the `site` prop: open and scroll to a linked host), `ui/src/admin/SiteDataPane.test.ts` (one base route)

**The drawing is the spec for the look:** Penpot *Admin UI*, page **Session · History**, boards `history`, `history-cleared`, `overlay-confirm-history-clear`. Its copy, exactly:
- the subtabs: `Files · Flows · Site data · History`, each with its count; History counts the hosts listed;
- above the rows, on the right, **Clear** (danger) — only while there is more than the current site;
- one card per host, the current site on top, then most recent first; always open, no fold, no marker on the top row. Its head: the latest URL in bold, `1m ago` in grey, and a pill with the saved counts for that host, zero counts left out (`2 cookies · 2 local`), which opens that host in Site data; no pill when nothing is saved there;
- under the head, one line per secret allowed on that host: `🔑 name`, its description, one pill per key — the Secrets tab's line;
- Clear's confirm: title `Clear history`; `History only: site data and the browser are untouched.`; `goes <host · host · …>`; `stays <current host>, the current site`; buttons `Cancel` / `Clear`.
- Not drawn: a session that has been nowhere. Use the session card's own words, `Nowhere yet.`, as the pane's empty line.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `GET /admin/sessions/{key}/history` → `{key, sites: [{site, url, at, saved: {cookies, local, session} | null, secrets: [{name, description, keys}]}]}`; `DELETE` the same; the session row's `history_count`, `history_rev` (Task 7).
- Produces:
  - `router`: `type Tab = 'files' | 'flows' | 'site-data' | 'history'`; a session route carries `site: string | undefined` (the fourth hash segment on Site data) beside `flow` (now only on Flows); `hashes.history(key)`, `hashes.site(key, host)` → `#/sessions/<key>/site-data/<host>`.
  - `SessionModel.history: HistoryPayload | null`, `historyError`, `loadHistory()`; a pushed row reloads it when `history_rev` moves.
  - `SessionDetail` takes `site?: string` and passes it to `SiteDataPane`, which opens that host's row and scrolls to it.

```ts
/** What the snapshot holds for one host, as History's pill counts it. */
export interface SavedCounts { cookies: number; local: number; session: number }
/** A host the session landed on: its latest page, and what can be used there. */
export interface HistoryRow {
  site: string
  url: string
  at: number
  saved: SavedCounts | null
  secrets: SiteSecret[]
}
export interface HistoryPayload { key: string; sites: HistoryRow[] }
```

`ui/src/admin/router.svelte.ts` — replace whole:

```ts
/* The hash, not the history API: every other path under this mount is a real
   endpoint, and a history route would need a catch-all that answers a mistyped
   API call with HTML. A hash never reaches the server. */
export type Tab = 'files' | 'flows' | 'site-data' | 'history'
export type Route =
  | { view: 'list' }
  | { view: 'console' }
  | { view: 'secrets' }
  | { view: 'settings' }
  | { view: 'session'; key: string; tab: Tab; flow: string | undefined; site: string | undefined }

const TABS: readonly string[] = ['flows', 'site-data', 'history']

export function parse(hash: string): Route {
  const [view, key, tab, rest] = hash.replace(/^#\/?/, '').split('/')
  if (view === 'console') return { view: 'console' }
  if (view === 'secrets') return { view: 'secrets' }
  if (view === 'settings') return { view: 'settings' }
  if (view === 'sessions' && key) {
    // parse() runs at module load: a bad pasted `%` must not stop the app booting.
    try {
      const picked = (TABS.includes(tab) ? tab : 'files') as Tab
      const named = rest ? decodeURIComponent(rest) : undefined
      return {
        view: 'session',
        key: decodeURIComponent(key),
        tab: picked,
        // The fourth segment is a flow on Flows and a host on Site data.
        flow: picked === 'flows' ? named : undefined,
        site: picked === 'site-data' ? named : undefined,
      }
    } catch {
      return { view: 'list' }
    }
  }
  return { view: 'list' }
}

const enc = encodeURIComponent
export const hashes = {
  list: '#/',
  console: '#/console',
  secrets: '#/secrets',
  settings: '#/settings',
  session: (key: string) => '#/sessions/' + enc(key),
  flows: (key: string) => '#/sessions/' + enc(key) + '/flows',
  siteData: (key: string) => '#/sessions/' + enc(key) + '/site-data',
  site: (key: string, host: string) => '#/sessions/' + enc(key) + '/site-data/' + enc(host),
  history: (key: string) => '#/sessions/' + enc(key) + '/history',
  flow: (key: string, name: string) => '#/sessions/' + enc(key) + '/flows/' + enc(name),
}

export const router = $state<{ route: Route }>({ route: parse(location.hash) })

export const go = (hash: string) => { location.hash = hash }

/* In the address bar and the route, without a history entry and without
   hashchange: picking through a dozen flows should not take a dozen Backs. */
export function replace(hash: string) {
  history.replaceState(null, '', hash)
  router.route = parse(hash)
}

/* The route, read from the address bar: at mount, and on every hashchange
   (Admin's `<svelte:window onhashchange>`). */
export function sync() {
  router.route = parse(location.hash)
}
```

```ts
  loadHistory(): Promise<void> {
    if (this.#disposed) return Promise.resolve()
    return this.#historyLoads.run(
      (signal) => this.#api<HistoryPayload>(sessionPath(this.key, '/history'), 'GET', undefined, signal),
      (data) => { this.history = data; this.historyError = null },
      (e) => {
        this.historyError = e.message
        // Unshown again, so the next push at the same rev tries once more.
        this.#shownHistory = undefined
      },
    )
  }
```

```svelte
<script lang="ts">
  import { ago } from '../lib/format'
  import type { SavedCounts } from '../lib/types'
  import { sessionPath, type Api } from './api'
  import type { ModalSpec } from './modal'
  import { go, hashes } from './router.svelte'
  import type { SessionModel } from './session.svelte'

  let { m, api, ask, refuseIfGone, isGone, hidden }: {
    m: SessionModel
    api: Api
    ask: <T>(spec: ModalSpec<T>) => void
    refuseIfGone: () => void
    isGone: () => boolean
    hidden: boolean
  } = $props()

  const data = $derived(m.history)

  // Zero counts are left out: "2 cookies · 2 local".
  const savedText = (s: SavedCounts) => [
    s.cookies ? s.cookies + (s.cookies === 1 ? ' cookie' : ' cookies') : '',
    s.local ? s.local + ' local' : '',
    s.session ? s.session + ' session' : '',
  ].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')

  interface Clear { goes: string[]; stays: string }

  function clear() {
    const sites = data?.sites ?? []
    ask<Clear>({
      title: 'Clear history',
      body: clearBody,
      data: { goes: sites.slice(1).map((r) => r.site), stays: sites[0]?.site ?? '' },
      confirm: 'Clear', danger: true,
      onconfirm: async () => {
        refuseIfGone()
        await api(sessionPath(m.key, '/history'), 'DELETE')
        if (!isGone()) void m.loadHistory()
      },
    })
  }
</script>

{#snippet clearBody(c: Clear)}
  <p>History only: site data and the browser are untouched.</p>
  <div class="pairs fate-list">
    <div class="pair"><span class="pk">goes</span><span class="pv">{c.goes.join(' · ')}</span></div>
    <div class="pair"><span class="pk">stays</span><span class="pv">{c.stays}, the current site</span></div>
  </div>
{/snippet}

<div id="paneHistory" {hidden}>
  {#if m.historyError}
    <div class="empty error">{m.historyError}</div>
  {:else if !data}
    <div class="empty">Loading…</div>
  {:else if !data.sites.length}
    <div class="empty">Nowhere yet.</div>
  {:else}
    {#if data.sites.length > 1}
      <div class="row bar">
        <span class="grow"></span>
        <button id="clearHistory" class="danger" onclick={clear}>Clear</button>
      </div>
    {/if}
    {#each data.sites as r (r.site)}
      {@const saved = r.saved ? savedText(r.saved) : ''}
      <!-- Always open: a visit has nothing to fold away. -->
      <section class="section visit">
        <div class="head">
          <strong class="url" title={r.url}>{r.url}</strong>
          <span class="small muted">{ago(r.at * 1000)}</span>
          {#if saved}
            <button class="pill link" onclick={() => go(hashes.site(m.key, r.site))}>{saved}</button>
          {/if}
        </div>
        {#if r.secrets.length}
          <div class="body">
            {#each r.secrets as s (s.name)}
              <div class="line secret">
                <span class="key"><span>🔑</span> <code>{s.name}</code></span>
                <span class="value">{s.description ?? ''}</span>
                <span class="flags">{#each s.keys as k (k)}<span class="pill">{k}</span>{/each}</span>
              </div>
            {/each}
          </div>
        {/if}
      </section>
    {/each}
  {/if}
</div>

<style>
  .bar { margin-bottom: 12px; }
  .url { font-weight: 600; overflow-wrap: anywhere; min-width: 0; }
  .pill.link { font: inherit; font-size: 12px; background: none; cursor: pointer; }
  .pill.link:hover { color: var(--accent); border-color: currentColor; }
  .line { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 240px 1fr auto; align-items: center; gap: 8px; padding: 6px 0; border-top: 1px solid var(--line); }
  .key { font-size: 12px; overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
  .value { overflow-wrap: anywhere; min-width: 0; }
  .flags { display: inline-flex; gap: 4px; justify-content: flex-end; }
  .fate-list { padding: 8px 10px; border-radius: 8px; background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ink) 5%, transparent); }
  .fate-list .pk { width: 48px; }
</style>
```

```ts
  // A link from History opens its host and brings it into view, once per link.
  let linked: string | undefined
  $effect.pre(() => {
    const r = (data?.sites ?? []).find((x) => x.site === site)
    if (!r || site === linked) return
    linked = site
    untrack(() => { folds[idOf(r)] = true })
    void tick().then(() => document.getElementById(idOf(r))?.scrollIntoView?.({ block: 'start' }))
  })
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Rewrite the skill's reference** — `skills/selenium-flow/references/SITE_DATA.md`, whole:

````markdown
# Site data — stay signed in across a reaped browser

A new browser starts empty. `save_site_data` keeps this one's cookies and the
storage of every site this session has been to, and every browser opened for the
session afterwards has them back — the one that replaces a reaped browser, or the
one after `end_browser()`.

## When to save

After a sign-in you have **confirmed**, never before: check the page shows you are
in, then save once.

```
write(url="https://app.example.com/login", selector={"css": "#user"}, text="a@example.com")
write(selector={"css": "#pw"}, secret={"name": "app", "key": "password"})
interact(action="click", selector={"css": "button[type=submit]"})
assert(script="return !!document.querySelector('#avatar')")   # am I in?
save_site_data()
```

One save covers every site the sign-in touched — the identity provider it went
through, and an app you signed in to earlier in the session. Save again after a
setting you want kept.

Each save **replaces** the last. A save after signing out saves you signed out.

The result names what was kept, never a value:

```json
{"saved": {"cookies": 14,
           "sites": ["https://app.example.com", "https://sso.example.com"],
           "skipped": []},
 "uri": "session://site-data"}
```

`skipped` names a site whose storage was not read this time; it keeps what the
last save had for it. "a service worker answered: save while on this site" means
the site's own worker answered first: `navigate` there and save again, and it is
read from the page itself.

## What comes back

Everything is in place before `open_session` returns: the cookies, every site's
localStorage, and the sessionStorage of the page you saved on. `open_session`
says which sites, and never a value:

```json
{"site_data": {"restored": ["app.example.com", "sso.example.com"], "skipped": [],
               "uri": "session://site-data"}}
```

No `site_data` key means nothing was saved. When the Grid reaps your browser, the
next call reopens it, restores the same way, and that call's result carries this
report once.

`skipped` names a cookie or a site that did not come back, and why. "the browser
did not keep it" means the browser took a cookie without an error and dropped it;
its host is then not in `restored`.

## Restored but still signed out

The site ended its own session — it expired or was revoked. Sign in again, then
`save_site_data()` again; nothing else is wrong.

## Start as a new user

```
open_session(restore_site_data=false)
```

Opens with nothing **and deletes** what was saved, so the next open is empty too.
Where the session has been is kept.

## Debugging with execute_script

Set or clear a flag to see what a reopened browser keeps:

```
execute_script(script="localStorage.setItem('seen', '1')")
save_site_data()
end_browser()
open_session()
execute_script(script="return localStorage.getItem('seen')")   # "1"
```

`document.cookie` shows what the page can see. `session://site-data/app.example.com`
shows what was saved, including httpOnly cookies the page cannot — their values
read `•••`, here and in the admin UI.

## Limits

- IndexedDB is not saved (Firebase Auth keeps its sign-in there).
- sessionStorage belongs to one tab, so only the page you save on keeps it.
- A browser opened with `insecure=true` gets none of it back: it accepts any
  certificate, so saved cookies could reach whoever sits in the middle. What was
  saved is kept for the next secure browser.
- A script cannot set an httpOnly cookie; it comes back through the browser's own
  channel.
- Stored on the session and expires with it. Never on this server's disk; with the
  Redis store it is as durable as Redis.
- A site that stores what you typed makes it readable in site data — a token typed
  through a secret and kept in localStorage shows in `session://site-data/{site}`.
  httpOnly cookies stay masked.
````

```markdown
site's storage, so you are still signed in — and the call that noticed says so
in `site_data` (`skill://selenium-flow/references/SITE_DATA.md`). What it cannot
restore is in-page state the URL does not capture — scroll position, an open
dropdown, an unsubmitted form — and anything you never saved. If a long pause is
coming before a step that depends on unsaved state, do that step first.
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The wiki's note** — `wiki/notes/save_site_data.notes.md`, whole:

```markdown
## When to save

Save once a sign-in is confirmed, not before: check the page shows you are in,
then call `save_site_data`. One save keeps every cookie, the localStorage of
every site the session has been to — read in a hidden tab that never loads the
site — and the sessionStorage of the page it is on. Each save replaces the last,
so a save after signing out saves you signed out.

## What restore does

Every browser opened for the session has it all back before `open_session`
returns, including the one that replaces a reaped browser: cookies first, then
each site's storage, then the page's sessionStorage, then the first page.
`open_session` names the sites in `site_data`; after a silent reopen, the call
that noticed carries that report once. `open_session(restore_site_data=false)`
opens with nothing and deletes what was saved, which is how you start as a new
user; where the session has been is kept.

## Limits

IndexedDB is not saved, and sessionStorage only for the page saved on. A site
whose service worker answers the hidden tab is not read there: it keeps its last
saved storage, and a save made while on it reads it directly. An httpOnly cookie
cannot be set from a script, so it is restored through the browser's own channel
and shown as `•••` on every surface. Saved data lives with the session's record
and expires with it: never on this server's disk, and with the Redis store as
durable as Redis.
```

- [ ] **Step 4: AGENTS.md** — replace the `### Site data` section (from its heading through the bullet that ends `fn` only computes, and may run twice.`, lines 398–410) with:

```markdown
### Site data

- Saved **explicitly** by `save_site_data`, never captured per call. A save is a **snapshot**
  that replaces the last whole (`site_data.snapshot`): the jar, the localStorage of every origin
  in the record's `history`, and the page's sessionStorage. An origin that cannot be read keeps
  its last storage; over 1 MB the oldest-visited origins go first.
- `SessionRecord.history` is where the session has been: one entry per origin, newest first,
  written by `touch` (a flow run: once at the end, every step's page in order — and, before a
  `save_site_data` step, the pages reached so far, so the save reads them). `record.url` is
  `history[0].url`. A withheld URL (§F1.24) or a page with no origin records nothing.
- Other origins are reached through `browser.spare_tab`: a background tab whose requests a BiDi
  intercept answers with a marked blank page, so the site never loads. A save reads there; a
  restore writes there, then sets sessionStorage in the main tab the same way, all before the
  first page. A page without the marker is a service worker's and is never read. No CDP.
- Kept on `SessionRecord` in the store, never on this server's disk (with the Redis store it is
  as durable as Redis), never logged — `main.py` holds Selenium's wire loggers at INFO for that;
  it expires with the record. httpOnly values are shown as `•••` on every surface.
- An action returns the capture under `CAPTURED`; `SessionManager.settle` stores it and strips
  it, so it is never returned. `act` and every flow step go through `settle`.
- A silent reopen's report waits on `SessionRecord.reopened` until `touch` hands it to the first
  result from that browser — for a flow, to the run.
- The admin tabs keep the two apart: History (the history joined by host with secrets and the
  snapshot's counts) and Site data (the snapshot). Forget and both Clears change the store
  only — never the live browser, never the other tab's data.
- Every record write goes through `store.update(key, fn)`, which applies `fn` to the record as
  it is at write time (memory: a per-key lock; Redis: `WATCH`/`MULTI`/`EXEC`, retried, then
  `StoreConflict`). A whole record read before slow work and `set` after reverts a browser
  opened or a save made meanwhile. Do slow work outside `fn`; `fn` only computes, and may run
  twice.
```

```bash
GRID_URL=http://selenium-grid-selenium-hub.flow.svc.cluster.local:4444 AUTH_TOKEN=probe-token \
  PORT=8766 PYTHONPATH=.:$S/deps python3 -S -m kubed.selenium_flow
```

and, in another shell, once per browser (`bash live.sh chrome`, then `bash live.sh firefox`):

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# live.sh <browser>: two apps signed in, one save, a real end and reopen.
B=$1; SID=live-$1; U=http://127.0.0.1:8766
H=(-s -m 180 -H "Authorization: Bearer probe-token" -H "Content-Type: application/json")
call(){ curl "${H[@]}" -X "$1" "$U$2?session=$SID" ${3:+-d "$3"}; echo; }
py(){ python3 -c "import sys,json; d=json.load(sys.stdin); print($1)"; }
ms(){ echo $(( ($(date +%s%N) - $1) / 1000000 )); }

echo "== $B: a clean start, then app 1 (an httpOnly cookie, and a localStorage mark)"
T=$(date +%s%N); call POST /browser "{\"browser\":\"$B\",\"url\":\"https://the-internet.herokuapp.com/login\",\"restore_site_data\":false}" >/dev/null; echo "plain open: $(ms $T) ms"
call POST /browser/write '{"selector":{"css":"#username"},"text":"tomsmith"}' >/dev/null
call POST /browser/write '{"selector":{"css":"#password"},"text":"SuperSecretPassword!"}' >/dev/null
call POST /browser/interact/click '{"selector":{"css":"button[type=submit]"}}' | py 'd.get("url")'
call POST /browser/script '{"script":"localStorage.setItem(\"probe\",\"kept\"); return true"}' >/dev/null

echo "== app 2 (a cookie sign-in, its cart in localStorage)"
call POST /browser/navigate '{"url":"https://www.saucedemo.com/"}' >/dev/null
call POST /browser/write '{"selector":{"css":"#user-name"},"text":"standard_user"}' >/dev/null
call POST /browser/write '{"selector":{"css":"#password"},"text":"secret_sauce"}' >/dev/null
call POST /browser/interact/click '{"selector":{"css":"#login-button"}}' | py 'd.get("url")'
call POST /browser/interact/click '{"selector":{"css":"#add-to-cart-sauce-labs-backpack"}}' >/dev/null

echo "== one save, on app 2 — sites must name both origins"
T=$(date +%s%N); call POST /browser/save-site-data '{}' | py 'd.get("saved"), "_site_data_captured" in d'; echo "save: $(ms $T) ms"

echo "== a real end and reopen"
call DELETE /browser >/dev/null
T=$(date +%s%N); call POST /browser '{}' | py 'd.get("url"), d.get("site_data")'; echo "open with restore: $(ms $T) ms"
call POST /browser/extract '{"url":"https://the-internet.herokuapp.com/secure","selector":{"css":"h2"}}' | py '"app 1 at", d.get("url")'
call POST /browser/script '{"script":"return localStorage.getItem(\"probe\")"}' | py '"app 1 localStorage:", d.get("result")'
call POST /browser/navigate '{"url":"https://www.saucedemo.com/inventory.html"}' | py '"app 2 at", d.get("url")'
call POST /browser/script '{"script":"return localStorage.getItem(\"cart-contents\")"}' | py '"app 2 cart:", d.get("result")'

echo "== the admin views"
curl "${H[@]}" "$U/admin/sessions/$SID/history" | py '[(s["site"], s["saved"]) for s in d["sites"]]'
curl "${H[@]}" "$U/admin/sessions/$SID/site-data" | py '[(s["site"], s["cookies"], s["storage"]) for s in d["sites"]]'

echo "== restore off: deleted, history kept"
call POST /browser '{"restore_site_data":false}' | py 'd.get("site_data")'
curl "${H[@]}" "$U/admin/sessions/$SID/site-data" | py '"site data after:", d["sites"]'
curl "${H[@]}" "$U/admin/sessions/$SID/history" | py '"history after:", [s["site"] for s in d["sites"]]'
call DELETE /browser >/dev/null
```

  Pass: the save's `saved.sites` names both `https://www.saucedemo.com` and `https://the-internet.herokuapp.com`; after the reopen app 1 stays on `/secure`, its probe reads `kept`, app 2 stays on `/inventory.html` with a cart, and `site_data.restored` names both hosts; `restore_site_data=false` answers `{"forgotten": 2}` (or more, with ad hosts), leaves Site data empty and History intact. Record both browsers' "open with restore" minus "plain open" — the restore's own cost; the spike predicts well under a second for a handful of origins. Then open the admin page on the live session and compare the two tabs with the Penpot boards.

# Round 1

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** A session saves its cookies, localStorage and sessionStorage with `save_site_data`, and every browser opened for it — the silent reopen after a reap included — comes back with them.

**Architecture:** Pure logic in a new `core/site_data.py` (merge, view, forget, the preload script). BiDi lives behind one `Grid.bidi(session_id)` context manager, used only by save and restore. `Actions` captures and restores; `SessionManager` merges into the record, announces restores, and deletes on `restore_site_data=false`. Surfaces, admin API and a Svelte pane sit on top.

**Tech Stack:** Python 3.10–3.14, Selenium 4.49 (WebDriver BiDi `storage` and `script` modules), FastMCP, Starlette, Svelte 5 + vitest.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-30-site-data-design.md` — read it before your task; it is the authority.

## Global Constraints

- The feature is called **site data**; the tool is `save_site_data`; the open argument is `restore_site_data` (default `true`); resources are `session://site-data` and `session://site-data/{site}`; the admin tab is **Site data**.
- Plain W3C WebDriver only. BiDi is allowed and used **only** for site data. No CDP.
- BiDi is reached per call: `Grid.bidi(session_id)` binds a reattached driver with `webSocketUrl = <GRID_URL with http→ws>/session/<id>/se/bidi` and closes its socket on exit.
- Site data lives on `SessionRecord.site_data` in the session store. Never on disk. Never logged. No new setting; it expires with the record.
- Size cap: `MAX_BYTES = 1_000_000` bytes of `json.dumps(site_data)`.
- httpOnly cookie values are shown as `"•••"` on every surface (resource, HTTP, admin). Nothing else is masked.
- Tab marker: sessionStorage key prefix `selenium-flow:restored:`; keys with it are never saved.
- The hint key in results is `site_data`; it appears **only** when something happened in that call.
- Every browser action is both a tool and an endpoint: `save_site_data` ↔ `POST /browser/save-site-data`.
- `SessionManager.act` is the only place a captured result is merged; the private result key is `CAPTURED = "_site_data_captured"` and never reaches a caller.
- House style: docstrings in `mcp/tools.py` are prompts; comments explain the non-obvious why and are terse; UI copy is terse, descriptions behind ⓘ, no helper sentences beyond the drawn ones.
- Tests: `PYTHONPATH=.:<deps> python3 -S -m pytest -q -m "not integration"` (the `-S` avoids kubed-krm shadowing the `kubed` namespace in the dev pod). Ruff clean. `test_wiki` may fail in the pod only because its subprocess lacks `-S`; `python3 -S scripts/generate_wiki.py --check` is the real check there.
- Never run psalm/php tools; never run integration tests in the pod.
- Commit messages end with `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.

## File Structure

| File | Responsibility |
|---|---|
| `kubed/selenium_flow/core/site_data.py` (new) | Pure: `origin_of`, `host_of`, `merge`, `live_cookies`, `preload_source`, `view`, `site_view`, `summary`, `forget`, `matching_secrets`. BiDi-using: `capture`, `restore`, `retire`. |
| `kubed/selenium_flow/core/browser.py` | `Grid._options` enables BiDi; `Grid.bidi(session_id)` context manager. |
| `kubed/selenium_flow/core/actions.py` | `save_site_data`, `open_session(site_data=...)`, `retire_site_data`. |
| `kubed/selenium_flow/session/store.py` | `SessionRecord.site_data`, JSON round trip, `with_site_data`. |
| `kubed/selenium_flow/session/sessions.py` | merge in `act`, hint on arrival, announce after reopen, `open_browser(restore_site_data)`, `remember` keeps site data, `describe` summary. |
| `kubed/selenium_flow/mcp/tools.py`, `routes.py`, `spec/schemas.py`, `mcp/resources.py` | The surface. |
| `kubed/selenium_flow/http/admin.py` | Admin API and session-row fields. |
| `ui/src/admin/SiteDataPane.svelte` (new), `SessionDetail.svelte`, `router.svelte.ts`, `ui/src/lib/types.ts` | The tab. |
| `skills/selenium-flow/references/SITE_DATA.md` (new) + existing references | The skill. |
| `tests/test_site_data.py` (new), `tests/test_site_data_sessions.py` (new), `tests/test_site_data_surface.py` (new), `ui/src/admin/SiteDataPane.test.ts` (new) | Tests. |
| `tests/integration/flows/save-site-data.yaml` (new) | The one integration flow. |

---

### Task 1: The model — pure site-data logic and the record field

**Files:**
- Create: `kubed/selenium_flow/core/site_data.py`
- Modify: `kubed/selenium_flow/session/store.py` (`SessionRecord`)
- Test: `tests/test_site_data.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces (used by every later task):
  - `CAPTURED = "_site_data_captured"`, `MARKER = "selenium-flow:restored:"`, `MAX_BYTES = 1_000_000`, `MASK = "•••"`
  - `origin_of(url: str) -> str` — `location.origin` form: `scheme://host[:port]`, lowercase host, default ports (http 80, https 443) dropped; `""` for anything without a scheme and host.
  - `host_of(origin_or_url: str) -> str` — lowercase hostname or `""`.
  - `merge(existing: dict, captured: dict, now: float) -> tuple[dict, dict]` — returns `(new_site_data, saved_summary)`.
  - `live_cookies(cookies: list[dict], now: float) -> list[dict]`
  - `preload_source(origins: dict[str, dict]) -> str`
  - `view(site_data: dict, secrets: list[dict] | None = None) -> dict` and `site_view(site_data: dict, site: str, secrets=None) -> dict | None`
  - `summary(site_data: dict) -> dict | None` — `{"sites": n, "uri": "session://site-data"}` or `None` when empty.
  - `forget(site_data: dict, host: str) -> tuple[dict, dict]`
  - `SessionRecord.site_data: dict` (default `{}`), carried by `at`, `reshaped`, `detached` (they use `dataclasses.replace`, so this is automatic), round-tripped by `to_json`/`from_json`, and `SessionRecord.with_site_data(site_data: dict) -> SessionRecord`.

Cookie dicts in `site_data["cookies"]` have exactly: `name, value, value_type ("string"|"base64"), domain, path, http_only, secure, same_site, expiry (int|None)`.

`captured` (what `Actions.save_site_data` produces, Task 2) is:
`{"cookies": [cookie dicts], "origin": "<location.origin or ''>", "local": {k: v}, "session": {k: v}}`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** — `tests/test_site_data.py`:

```python
"""Site data: what a session saves for the sites it signed in to, and how
it is shown. Pure logic only; BiDi is Task 2's."""

import json

import pytest

from kubed.selenium_flow.core import site_data as sd
from kubed.selenium_flow.session.store import SessionRecord

pytestmark = pytest.mark.unit

NOW = 1_790_800_000.0


def cookie(name, domain, value="v", http_only=False, expiry=None, secure=True):
    return {
        "name": name, "value": value, "value_type": "string", "domain": domain,
        "path": "/", "http_only": http_only, "secure": secure,
        "same_site": "lax", "expiry": expiry,
    }


def captured(origin="https://app.example.com", local=None, session=None, cookies=None):
    return {
        "cookies": cookies if cookies is not None else [cookie("sid", "app.example.com")],
        "origin": origin, "local": local or {}, "session": session or {},
    }


def test_origin_of_matches_location_origin():
    assert sd.origin_of("https://App.Example.com:443/x?y#z") == "https://app.example.com"
    assert sd.origin_of("http://localhost:3000/a") == "http://localhost:3000"
    assert sd.origin_of("http://x.test:80/") == "http://x.test"
    assert sd.origin_of("about:blank") == ""
    assert sd.origin_of("") == ""


def test_a_save_replaces_cookies_and_adds_its_origin():
    first, _ = sd.merge({}, captured(local={"theme": "dark"}), NOW)
    second, saved = sd.merge(
        first,
        captured(origin="https://sso.example.com", cookies=[cookie("kc", "sso.example.com")]),
        NOW + 5,
    )
    assert [c["name"] for c in second["cookies"]] == ["kc"], "cookies are the whole jar, replaced"
    assert set(second["origins"]) == {"https://app.example.com", "https://sso.example.com"}
    assert second["origins"]["https://app.example.com"]["local"] == {"theme": "dark"}
    assert saved == {"cookies": 1, "sites": ["https://sso.example.com"], "skipped": []}


def test_a_page_with_no_origin_saves_cookies_only():
    data, saved = sd.merge({}, captured(origin=""), NOW)
    assert data["origins"] == {}
    assert saved["sites"] == []


def test_the_restore_marker_is_never_saved():
    data, _ = sd.merge(
        {}, captured(session={"selenium-flow:restored:https://app.example.com": "1", "k": "v"}), NOW
    )
    assert data["origins"]["https://app.example.com"]["session"] == {"k": "v"}


def test_a_save_over_the_cap_keeps_cookies_and_skips_that_storage():
    big = {"blob": "x" * (sd.MAX_BYTES + 1)}
    data, saved = sd.merge({}, captured(local=big), NOW)
    assert data["cookies"] and data["origins"] == {}
    assert saved["skipped"] == [
        {"site": "https://app.example.com", "reason": "storage over 1000000 bytes"}
    ]


def test_merge_keeps_pending():
    data, _ = sd.merge({"pending": {"browser": "b", "origins": [], "script": ""}}, captured(), NOW)
    assert data["pending"]["browser"] == "b"


def test_expired_cookies_are_dropped_and_session_cookies_kept():
    kept = sd.live_cookies(
        [cookie("old", "a.test", expiry=int(NOW) - 1), cookie("new", "a.test", expiry=int(NOW) + 60),
         cookie("session", "a.test", expiry=None)],
        NOW,
    )
    assert [c["name"] for c in kept] == ["new", "session"]


def test_the_preload_script_fills_only_its_own_origin_once_per_tab():
    src = sd.preload_source({"https://app.example.com": {"local": {"a": "1"}, "session": {"b": "2"}}})
    assert src.startswith("() =>")
    assert "location.origin" in src
    assert "selenium-flow:restored:" in src
    assert json.dumps({"a": "1"}) in src


def test_the_view_groups_by_host_and_shares_parent_cookies():
    data, _ = sd.merge(
        {},
        captured(
            local={"theme": "dark"},
            cookies=[
                cookie("sid", "app.example.com", value="secret", http_only=True),
                cookie("ab", ".example.com", value="b1"),
                cookie("kc", "sso.example.com"),
            ],
        ),
        NOW,
    )
    listing = sd.view(data)
    sites = {s["site"]: s for s in listing["sites"]}
    assert set(sites) == {"app.example.com", "sso.example.com"}
    assert sites["app.example.com"]["origin"] == "https://app.example.com"
    assert sites["app.example.com"]["cookies"] == 2, "its own and the shared one"
    assert sites["sso.example.com"]["cookies"] == 2
    assert sites["app.example.com"]["uri"] == "session://site-data/app.example.com"
    one = sd.site_view(data, "app.example.com")
    by_name = {c["name"]: c for c in one["cookies"]}
    assert by_name["sid"]["value"] == sd.MASK, "httpOnly is masked"
    assert by_name["ab"]["value"] == "b1" and by_name["ab"]["shared"] is True
    assert one["local_storage"] == {"theme": "dark"}
    assert sd.site_view(data, "nope.test") is None


def test_a_parent_cookie_with_no_host_gets_its_own_site():
    data, _ = sd.merge({}, captured(origin="", cookies=[cookie("ab", ".example.com")]), NOW)
    assert [s["site"] for s in sd.view(data)["sites"]] == ["example.com"]


def test_secrets_are_matched_by_host_and_unleashed_ones_counted():
    secrets = [
        {"name": "app", "description": "d", "keys": ["username", "password"],
         "allowed_urls": ["https://app.example.com"], "restricted": True},
        {"name": "anywhere", "description": "", "keys": ["token"], "allowed_urls": [],
         "restricted": False},
        {"name": "admin", "description": "a", "keys": ["token"],
         "allowed_urls": ["https://admin.example.com"], "restricted": True},
    ]
    data, _ = sd.merge({}, captured(), NOW)
    listing = sd.view(data, secrets)
    sites = {s["site"]: s for s in listing["sites"]}
    assert sites["app.example.com"]["secrets"] == [
        {"name": "app", "description": "d", "keys": ["username", "password"]}
    ]
    assert sites["admin.example.com"]["saved"] is False, "a secret keeps a row with nothing saved"
    assert listing["unleashed_secrets"] == 1
    assert listing["saved_sites"] == 1


def test_forget_keeps_shared_cookies_and_other_sites():
    data, _ = sd.merge(
        {},
        captured(cookies=[cookie("sid", "app.example.com"), cookie("ab", ".example.com"),
                          cookie("kc", "sso.example.com")]),
        NOW,
    )
    left, removed = sd.forget(data, "app.example.com")
    assert [c["name"] for c in left["cookies"]] == ["ab", "kc"]
    assert left["origins"] == {}
    assert removed == {"site": "app.example.com", "cookies": ["sid"], "origins": ["https://app.example.com"],
                       "kept_shared": ["ab"]}


def test_summary_is_none_when_empty():
    assert sd.summary({}) is None
    data, _ = sd.merge({}, captured(), NOW)
    assert sd.summary(data) == {"sites": 1, "uri": "session://site-data"}


def test_the_record_round_trips_its_site_data():
    data, _ = sd.merge({}, captured(), NOW)
    record = SessionRecord(session_id="s", url="u").with_site_data(data)
    again = SessionRecord.from_json(record.to_json())
    assert again.site_data == data
    assert again.detached().site_data == data, "ending a browser keeps site data"
    assert again.at("https://x").site_data == data


def test_a_record_written_before_site_data_reads_as_empty():
    assert SessionRecord.from_json('{"session_id": "s", "url": "u"}').site_data == {}
    assert SessionRecord.from_json('{"session_id": "s", "site_data": [1]}').site_data == {}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** — `kubed/selenium_flow/core/site_data.py`:

```python
"""Site data: the cookies and storage a session keeps for the sites it uses.

A session outlives its browser, and used to come back signed out: the page it
was on survived a reap, the sign-in did not. An agent now saves a site's data
when it knows it is worth keeping — after a sign-in is confirmed — and every
browser opened for the session gets it back (spec 2026-09-30).

Saved, not captured: every call paying for a snapshot was rejected, and a reap
gives no warning, so only an explicit save is dependable.

**A site is a host.** Cookies carry a domain and no scheme or port, so the view
groups by host. Storage is kept per origin, keyed by the page's own
``location.origin`` because that is the string the preload script compares.

Values are credentials. They live in the session store and nowhere else, are
never logged, and an httpOnly cookie's value is never shown on any surface.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from urllib.parse import urlsplit

# The private key an action hands its capture back under. `SessionManager.act`
# merges it into the record and removes it; no caller ever sees it.
CAPTURED = "_site_data_captured"
# Marks a tab whose storage was already filled, so a later page on the same
# origin never overwrites what the app changed since. Never saved.
MARKER = "selenium-flow:restored:"
MAX_BYTES = 1_000_000
MASK = "•••"
LIST_URI = "session://site-data"
DEFAULT_PORTS = {"http": 80, "https": 443}


def origin_of(url: str) -> str:
    """``url``'s origin as ``location.origin`` spells it, or "" when it has none."""
    try:
        parts = urlsplit((url or "").strip())
        host = (parts.hostname or "").lower()
        port = parts.port
    except ValueError:
        return ""
    if not parts.scheme or not host:
        return ""
    scheme = parts.scheme.lower()
    shown = f":{port}" if port is not None and port != DEFAULT_PORTS.get(scheme) else ""
    return f"{scheme}://{host}{shown}"


def host_of(url: str) -> str:
    try:
        return (urlsplit((url or "").strip()).hostname or "").lower()
    except ValueError:
        return ""


def site_uri(host: str) -> str:
    return f"{LIST_URI}/{host}"


def merge(existing: dict, captured: dict, now: float) -> tuple[dict, dict]:
    """Fold one save into what the session already had.

    The cookies are the whole jar and replace the last save's; storage is the
    one origin the page was on, added beside the others. A save that would
    pass the cap keeps its cookies and leaves that origin's storage out,
    saying so, rather than failing or truncating.
    """
    data = {
        "cookies": list(captured.get("cookies") or []),
        "origins": dict((existing or {}).get("origins") or {}),
        "saved_at": now,
    }
    if (existing or {}).get("pending"):
        data["pending"] = existing["pending"]
    origin = captured.get("origin") or ""
    sites, skipped = [], []
    if origin:
        entry = {
            "local": dict(captured.get("local") or {}),
            "session": {
                k: v for k, v in (captured.get("session") or {}).items()
                if not k.startswith(MARKER)
            },
            "saved_at": now,
        }
        trial = {**data, "origins": {**data["origins"], origin: entry}}
        if len(json.dumps(trial)) > MAX_BYTES:
            skipped.append({"site": origin, "reason": f"storage over {MAX_BYTES} bytes"})
        else:
            data = trial
            sites.append(origin)
    return data, {"cookies": len(data["cookies"]), "sites": sites, "skipped": skipped}


def live_cookies(cookies: list[dict], now: float) -> list[dict]:
    """Every cookie a restore should set: expired ones dropped, session
    cookies (no expiry) kept, because the session they belong to is ours and
    outlived the browser."""
    return [c for c in cookies if c.get("expiry") is None or c["expiry"] > now]


def preload_source(origins: dict[str, dict]) -> str:
    """The script that fills each origin's storage before the page's own
    scripts run, once per tab. Every origin rides in one script because a
    preload script cannot be scoped to a URL."""
    payload = json.dumps(
        {o: {"local": e.get("local") or {}, "session": e.get("session") or {}}
         for o, e in origins.items()}
    )
    marker = json.dumps(MARKER)
    return (
        "() => { const all = " + payload + "; const o = location.origin; "
        "const s = all[o]; if (!s) return; const m = " + marker + " + o; "
        "try { if (sessionStorage.getItem(m)) return; "
        "for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(s.local)) localStorage.setItem(k, v); "
        "for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(s.session)) sessionStorage.setItem(k, v); "
        "sessionStorage.setItem(m, '1'); } catch (e) {} }"
    )


def _covers(domain: str, host: str) -> bool:
    bare = domain.lstrip(".")
    return host == bare or (domain.startswith(".") and host.endswith(domain))


def _hosts(data: dict) -> list[str]:
    stored = {host_of(o) for o in (data.get("origins") or {})}
    for c in data.get("cookies") or []:
        domain = c.get("domain") or ""
        if domain and not any(_covers(domain, h) for h in stored):
            stored.add(domain.lstrip("."))
    return sorted(h for h in stored if h)


def _cookie_view(c: dict) -> dict:
    return {
        "name": c.get("name"),
        "value": MASK if c.get("http_only") else c.get("value"),
        "domain": c.get("domain"),
        "path": c.get("path"),
        "expiry": c.get("expiry"),
        "http_only": bool(c.get("http_only")),
        "secure": bool(c.get("secure")),
        "same_site": c.get("same_site"),
        "shared": (c.get("domain") or "").startswith("."),
    }


def matching_secrets(secrets: list[dict] | None, host: str) -> list[dict]:
    """The secrets allowed on ``host``, names and keys only. A secret with no
    `allowed_urls` is usable anywhere and is not listed under every site."""
    return [
        {"name": s["name"], "description": s.get("description") or "", "keys": list(s.get("keys") or [])}
        for s in secrets or []
        if any(host_of(u) == host for u in s.get("allowed_urls") or [])
    ]


def _site(data: dict, host: str, secrets) -> dict:
    origins = {o: e for o, e in (data.get("origins") or {}).items() if host_of(o) == host}
    cookies = [c for c in data.get("cookies") or [] if _covers(c.get("domain") or "", host)]
    local, session = {}, {}
    for entry in origins.values():
        local.update(entry.get("local") or {})
        session.update(entry.get("session") or {})
    saved = [e.get("saved_at") for e in origins.values() if e.get("saved_at")]
    return {
        "site": host,
        "origin": next(iter(sorted(origins)), None),
        "saved": bool(origins or cookies),
        "saved_at": max(saved) if saved else (data.get("saved_at") if cookies else None),
        "uri": site_uri(host),
        "_cookies": cookies,
        "_local": local,
        "_session": session,
        "secrets": matching_secrets(secrets, host),
    }


def view(data: dict, secrets: list[dict] | None = None) -> dict:
    """The listing: one entry per site, counts only, never a value.

    With ``secrets`` (the admin catalogue listing), a site a secret is allowed
    on is listed even with nothing saved — secrets are not ephemeral, so their
    rows are not either.
    """
    hosts = set(_hosts(data or {}))
    for s in secrets or []:
        hosts.update(host_of(u) for u in s.get("allowed_urls") or [])
    rows = []
    for host in sorted(h for h in hosts if h):
        site = _site(data or {}, host, secrets)
        rows.append({
            "site": site["site"], "origin": site["origin"], "saved": site["saved"],
            "saved_at": site["saved_at"], "uri": site["uri"],
            "cookies": len(site["_cookies"]), "local_storage": len(site["_local"]),
            "session_storage": len(site["_session"]), "secrets": site["secrets"],
        })
    unleashed = sum(1 for s in secrets or [] if not s.get("allowed_urls"))
    return {
        "sites": rows,
        "saved_sites": sum(1 for r in rows if r["saved"]),
        "unleashed_secrets": unleashed,
        "uri": LIST_URI,
    }


def site_view(data: dict, site: str, secrets: list[dict] | None = None) -> dict | None:
    """One site in full: cookies (httpOnly masked) and both storages."""
    host = (site or "").lower()
    listed = {r["site"] for r in view(data, secrets)["sites"]}
    if host not in listed:
        return None
    s = _site(data or {}, host, secrets)
    return {
        "site": host, "origin": s["origin"], "saved": s["saved"], "saved_at": s["saved_at"],
        "uri": s["uri"], "cookies": [_cookie_view(c) for c in s["_cookies"]],
        "local_storage": s["_local"], "session_storage": s["_session"], "secrets": s["secrets"],
    }


def summary(data: dict) -> dict | None:
    sites = _hosts(data or {})
    return {"sites": len(sites), "uri": LIST_URI} if sites else None


def forget(data: dict, host: str) -> tuple[dict, dict]:
    """Remove one site: its origins and the cookies that are its own. A
    parent-domain cookie stays — other sites use it."""
    host = (host or "").lower()
    cookies = data.get("cookies") or []
    gone = [c for c in cookies if (c.get("domain") or "") == host]
    shared = [c for c in cookies if (c.get("domain") or "").startswith(".") and _covers(c["domain"], host)]
    origins = [o for o in (data.get("origins") or {}) if host_of(o) == host]
    left = {
        **data,
        "cookies": [c for c in cookies if c not in gone],
        "origins": {o: e for o, e in (data.get("origins") or {}).items() if o not in origins},
    }
    return left, {
        "site": host, "cookies": [c["name"] for c in gone], "origins": origins,
        "kept_shared": [c["name"] for c in shared],
    }
```

### Task 2: BiDi — open with it, save through it, restore through it

**Files:**
- Modify: `kubed/selenium_flow/core/browser.py` (`Grid._options`, new `Grid.bidi`)
- Modify: `kubed/selenium_flow/core/site_data.py` (add `capture`, `restore`, `retire`)
- Modify: `kubed/selenium_flow/core/actions.py` (`save_site_data`, `open_session(site_data=None)`, `retire_site_data`)
- Test: `tests/test_site_data.py` (append)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1's `merge`, `live_cookies`, `preload_source`, `origin_of`, `host_of`, `CAPTURED`, `MARKER`.
- Produces:
  - `Grid.bidi(session_id: str)` — a context manager yielding a reattached driver with `.storage` and `.script`; the socket is closed on exit. `webSocketUrl` = `self.url` with its scheme `http`→`ws` (`https`→`wss`) + `/session/{session_id}/se/bidi`.
  - `site_data.capture(bidi, driver) -> dict` — the `captured` shape from Task 1. `driver` is the classic reattached driver on the page; storage is read with one `execute_script`.
  - `site_data.restore(bidi, data: dict, landing_url: str | None, now: float) -> tuple[dict, dict]` → `(report, pending)`. `report = {"restored": [...hosts/origins], "waiting": [origins], "skipped": [{"cookie": name, "domain": d, "reason": str}]}`; `pending = {"origins": [...], "script": "<id or ''>"}`.
  - `site_data.retire(bidi, script: str) -> None` — best effort.
  - `Actions.save_site_data(session_id: str, url: str | None = None) -> dict` → `{"url", "title", CAPTURED: captured}`.
  - `Actions.open_session(..., site_data: dict | None = None)` → adds `"site_data": report` and `"_site_data_pending": pending` to its result **only when** `site_data` had anything.
  - `Actions.retire_site_data(session_id: str, script: str) -> None`.

**Restore rules** (from the spec): set every live cookie with `storage.set_cookie(PartialCookie(name, BytesValue(value_type, value), domain, path=, http_only=, secure=, same_site=, expiry=))`; a failure appends to `skipped` and continues. If any origin has storage, add one preload script with `script.add_preload_script(preload_source(origins))`; its id is `result["script"]` when the result is a dict, else the result. `restored` = the landing URL's origin first, if it has storage, then every cookie-only host (a host with cookies and no stored origin) sorted; `waiting` = every other stored origin, sorted. Nothing raises out of `restore`; a BiDi failure as a whole returns `{"restored": [], "waiting": [], "skipped": [{"reason": "<message>"}]}` and empty pending.

**Capture script** (the `execute_script` body):

```python
READ_STORAGE = (
    "const dump = (s) => { const out = {}; try { for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i++) "
    "{ const k = s.key(i); out[k] = s.getItem(k); } } catch (e) {} return out; }; "
    "return {origin: location.origin === 'null' ? '' : location.origin, "
    "local: dump(localStorage), session: dump(sessionStorage)};"
)
```

Cookie dicts from BiDi `StorageCookie`: `value_type = c.value.type`, `value = c.value.value`, `same_site = c.same_site`, `expiry = c.expiry`.

```python
class FakeStorage:
    def __init__(self, cookies=(), refuse=()):
        self.cookies, self.set, self.refuse = list(cookies), [], set(refuse)

    def get_cookies(self, filter=None, partition=None):
        from types import SimpleNamespace
        return SimpleNamespace(cookies=self.cookies)

    def set_cookie(self, cookie=None, partition=None):
        if cookie.name in self.refuse:
            raise RuntimeError("unable to set cookie")
        self.set.append(cookie)


class FakeScript:
    def __init__(self):
        self.added, self.removed = [], []

    def add_preload_script(self, function_declaration=None, **_):
        self.added.append(function_declaration)
        return {"script": "preload-1"}

    def remove_preload_script(self, script=None):
        self.removed.append(script)


class FakeBidi:
    def __init__(self, **kw):
        self.storage, self.script = FakeStorage(**kw), FakeScript()


def bidi_cookie(name, domain, value="v", http_only=False):
    from types import SimpleNamespace
    return SimpleNamespace(name=name, domain=domain, path="/", http_only=http_only, secure=True,
                           same_site="lax", expiry=None,
                           value=SimpleNamespace(type="string", value=value))


class PageDriver:
    def __init__(self, dump):
        self.dump = dump

    def execute_script(self, script, *args):
        return self.dump


def test_capture_reads_every_cookie_and_this_page_storage():
    bidi = FakeBidi(cookies=[bidi_cookie("sid", "app.example.com", http_only=True),
                             bidi_cookie("kc", "sso.example.com")])
    got = sd.capture(bidi, PageDriver({"origin": "https://app.example.com",
                                       "local": {"a": "1"}, "session": {}}))
    assert [c["name"] for c in got["cookies"]] == ["sid", "kc"]
    assert got["cookies"][0] == {"name": "sid", "value": "v", "value_type": "string",
                                 "domain": "app.example.com", "path": "/", "http_only": True,
                                 "secure": True, "same_site": "lax", "expiry": None}
    assert got["origin"] == "https://app.example.com" and got["local"] == {"a": "1"}


def test_restore_sets_live_cookies_and_one_preload_script():
    data, _ = sd.merge({}, captured(local={"a": "1"},
                                    cookies=[cookie("sid", "app.example.com"),
                                             cookie("kc", "sso.example.com"),
                                             cookie("old", "app.example.com", expiry=int(NOW) - 1)]), NOW)
    data, _ = sd.merge(data, captured(origin="https://other.example.com", local={"b": "2"},
                                      cookies=data["cookies"]), NOW)
    bidi = FakeBidi()
    report, pending = sd.restore(bidi, data, "https://app.example.com/home", NOW)
    assert [c.name for c in bidi.storage.set] == ["sid", "kc"], "expired cookie not replayed"
    assert len(bidi.script.added) == 1
    assert report["restored"] == ["https://app.example.com", "sso.example.com"]
    assert report["waiting"] == ["https://other.example.com"]
    assert pending == {"origins": ["https://other.example.com"], "script": "preload-1"}


def test_a_refused_cookie_is_skipped_not_fatal():
    data, _ = sd.merge({}, captured(cookies=[cookie("bad", "app.example.com"),
                                             cookie("ok", "app.example.com")]), NOW)
    bidi = FakeBidi(refuse={"bad"})
    report, _ = sd.restore(bidi, data, None, NOW)
    assert [c.name for c in bidi.storage.set] == ["ok"]
    assert report["skipped"] == [{"cookie": "bad", "domain": "app.example.com",
                                  "reason": "unable to set cookie"}]


def test_restore_with_no_storage_adds_no_preload_script():
    data, _ = sd.merge({}, captured(origin=""), NOW)
    bidi = FakeBidi()
    _, pending = sd.restore(bidi, data, None, NOW)
    assert bidi.script.added == [] and pending == {"origins": [], "script": ""}

    for name in ("chrome", "firefox"):
        assert Grid()._options(name).to_capabilities().get("webSocketUrl") is True
```

### Task 3: The session — merge, announce, delete

**Files:**
- Modify: `kubed/selenium_flow/session/sessions.py`
- Test: `tests/test_site_data_sessions.py` (new)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1 (`merge`, `origin_of`, `site_uri`, `host_of`, `summary`, `CAPTURED`, `SessionRecord.with_site_data`), Task 2 (`Actions.open_session(site_data=)` result keys `site_data` / `_site_data_pending`, `Actions.retire_site_data`).
- Produces:
  - `SessionManager.act`: pops `CAPTURED` from a dict result, merges it into the record (`merge(record.site_data, captured, time.time())`), and puts `"saved": <summary>, "uri": "session://site-data"` in the result. After `touch`, if the record's `site_data["pending"]` names **this** browser and the result URL's origin is in `pending["origins"]`, the result gets `"site_data": {"restored": [origin], "uri": site_uri(host_of(origin))}` and the origin leaves `pending`; when `pending["origins"]` becomes empty and `pending["script"]` is set, call `self.actions.retire_site_data(session_id, script)` and clear `script`. If `pending["announce"]` is true (a silent reopen happened), the result gets `"site_data": <the reopen report>` (merged with any arrival above) and `announce` is cleared.
  - `SessionManager.open_browser(name, url=None, fresh=False, restore_site_data=True, **wanted)`: with restore on and saved data present, passes `site_data=record.site_data` to `actions.open_session`; stores `pending = {"browser": new_id, **result.pop("_site_data_pending"), "announce": False}` in the record's site data; returns the `site_data` report. With restore off and saved data present: opens without it, **deletes** it (`with_site_data({})`) and returns `"site_data": {"forgotten": <number of sites per summary>}`. With nothing saved, no `site_data` key at all.
  - `resolve`: the reopen passes `site_data=record.site_data` when it has any, stores `pending` with `"announce": True` and `"report": <report>`.
  - `remember(name, session_id, url="", settings=None)` keeps the existing record's `site_data` (read it before writing the new record).
  - `describe`: adds `"site_data": summary(record.site_data)` when not `None`.

Use `as_bool(restore_site_data, True)` — callers send JSON by hand.

**Tool docstrings (prompts, verbatim):**

`save_site_data`:
```
Save this session's site data: every cookie the browser holds, and the
localStorage and sessionStorage of the page it is on. Call it right after a
sign-in is confirmed, and after changing a setting you want kept; never before
checking you landed, or you keep a failed sign-in.

Every browser opened for this session gets it back, the one that replaces a
reaped browser included, so you come back signed in. Saving replaces the
cookies and adds this page's storage beside other sites'. Values are never
returned; session://site-data lists what is saved.
```

`open_session` — add after the `fresh` sentence:
```
Saved site data comes back by default; restore_site_data=false opens without it
and deletes it, which is how you start as a new user.
```

### Task 5: The admin API

**Files:**
- Modify: `kubed/selenium_flow/http/admin.py`
- Test: `tests/test_admin_site_data.py` (new)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1 `view`, `site_view`, `forget`, `summary`; `catalogue.listing()["secrets"]` (entries have `name, description, keys, allowed_urls, restricted`).
- Produces:
  - `GET {prefix}/admin/sessions/{key}/site-data` → `{"key": key, **site_data.view(record.site_data, secrets), "details": {host: site_view(...) for every listed site}}`. `secrets` is `catalogue.listing()["secrets"]` or `[]` when there is no catalogue. A key with no record answers the same shape with nothing saved (secret rows still listed).
  - `DELETE {prefix}/admin/sessions/{key}/site-data/{site}` → `{"forgotten": <forget's removed summary>}`; writes `record.with_site_data(left)` back; 404 `{"error": "no saved site data for <site>"}` when the site has nothing saved. Clears the host's origins from `pending["origins"]` too.
  - Every session row in `sessions_payload()` gains `"site_data_count": view(record.site_data)["saved_sites"]` (no secrets — the count is saved sites only) and `"site_data_rev": json.dumps(<sorted [host, saved_at, cookies] per saved site>)`.
  - Both routes are `@guarded` (token), like `/admin/sessions/{key}/files`.
- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** — list shape with a secret-only row; details mask httpOnly; DELETE keeps shared cookies and the secret row, and 404s the second time; unauthenticated GET is 401; `site_data_count` and `site_data_rev` change after a DELETE.
- [ ] **Step 2–4:** red, implement, green (`…pytest -q tests/test_admin_site_data.py tests/test_admin_files.py tests/test_files_and_admin.py`).

### Task 6: The Site data tab

**Files:**
- Create: `ui/src/admin/SiteDataPane.svelte`, `ui/src/admin/SiteDataPane.test.ts`
- Modify: `ui/src/admin/router.svelte.ts` (tab `'site-data'`, `hashes.siteData(key)` → `#/sessions/<key>/site-data`), `ui/src/admin/SessionDetail.svelte` (third subtab + pane), `ui/src/lib/types.ts` (types for the admin payload), `ui/src/admin/router.test.ts`

**The drawing is the spec for the look:** Penpot *Admin UI*, page **Session · Site data**, version *Site data design, round 1 — secret key pills*; boards `site-data`, `site-data-forgotten`, `site-data-nothing-saved`, `overlay-confirm-site-forget`. Reuse the existing pieces the drawing reuses: the `Section` accordion rows (as `FilesPane` uses them), the Settings-style key/value rows, pills, the danger button and the confirm `Modal`. Read `FilesPane.svelte`, `SettingsPane.svelte`, `SecretsPane.svelte` and `Section.svelte` first and copy their idioms.

**Behaviour:**
- Subtab label `Site data` with the count from the session row's `site_data_count`.
- Loads `GET /admin/sessions/{key}/site-data`; reloads when the session row's `site_data_rev` changes (the way Files follows `files_rev`).
- One row per `sites[]` entry: title = `origin ?? site`; a `saved <relative time>` pill when `saved_at`; grey counts `N cookies · N local · N session · N secret(s)`; `Forget` (danger) only when `saved`. Nothing saved: counts read `nothing saved · N secret`.
- Expanded: `COOKIES` rows — mono name, value (dots for httpOnly as the API sends them), grey `domain · expiry` (expiry as the page formats dates elsewhere, `session` when null), pills `httpOnly`, `secure`, and a blue `shared`; `LOCAL STORAGE` and `SESSION STORAGE` rows — mono key, value, `none` when empty; `SECRETS` rows — `🔑 name`, description, **one pill per key name**. A row with no saved data shows "Nothing saved. It stays listed because a secret is allowed here." above SECRETS.
- Above the rows when `saved_sites === 0`: `Nothing saved — an agent calls save_site_data after signing in.` with `save_site_data` in mono.
- Forget opens the confirm: title `Forget site data`; body `<origin> — a reopened browser comes back signed out here.`; a mono list of `goes <names…>` then one `stays` line per kept shared cookie and per secret; buttons `Cancel` / `Forget`; confirming calls DELETE and reloads.
- No restore on/off pill.

### Task 7: The skill, the docs, and the integration flow

**Files:**
- Create: `skills/selenium-flow/references/SITE_DATA.md`, `tests/integration/flows/save-site-data.yaml`
- Modify: `skills/selenium-flow/SKILL.md`, `references/SESSIONS.md`, `references/SECRETS.md`, `references/FLOWS.md`, `references/TROUBLESHOOTING.md`, `AGENTS.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`

**Content:**
- `SITE_DATA.md` — sections: *When to save* (after a confirmed sign-in: extract/assert, then `save_site_data()`; after a setting you want kept; never before checking); *What comes back* (cookies at once, storage on arrival; reading `site_data` in `open_session`'s result — `waiting` is normal); *Restored but still signed out* (the site ended its own session; sign in, then save again); *Start as a new user* (`open_session(restore_site_data=false)` deletes); *Debugging with execute_script* (set/clear a localStorage flag; read `document.cookie`; compare with `session://site-data/{site}` for what the page cannot see); *Limits* (IndexedDB; httpOnly cookies cannot be set by a script; saving replaces all cookies, so a save after signing out saves you signed out). Terse, example-led, in the voice of the other references.
- `SKILL.md` — "State carries over" gains "and across a reaped browser, if you saved it"; one row in the "Where to go next" table for SITE_DATA.md; the minimal task example ends with `save_site_data()` before `end_browser()`; `save_site_data` in the tool list.
- `SESSIONS.md` — the reap section: a refresh restores saved site data; "cannot restore" keeps only in-page state.
- `SECRETS.md` — the sign-in example ends with `save_site_data()`.
- `FLOWS.md` — a login flow ends with a `save_site_data` step.
- `TROUBLESHOOTING.md` — "Signed in yesterday, signed out today" → SITE_DATA.md.
- `AGENTS.md` — the "Plain W3C WebDriver only" rule names BiDi as W3C and allowed, used only for site data, reattached per call via `Grid.bidi`; a short "Site data" section: saved explicitly, never captured per call; kept on the record, never on disk; httpOnly never shown; the `CAPTURED` key is merged in `act` and never returned.
- `CHANGELOG.md` `[Unreleased]` — one line each: `save_site_data` keeps a session's cookies and storage so a reopened browser comes back signed in; `open_session(restore_site_data=false)` starts without it and deletes it; the admin UI has a Site data tab.
- `save-site-data.yaml` — same parameters as `keep-a-screenshot.yaml`; steps: navigate `${admin}/`, write the admin token (secret `admin`/`token`) into `#token`, click the login submit, `save_site_data`, navigate `${admin}/#/sessions/${session}/site-data`, `assert` that the row for the admin origin's host lists the key `sf-token` under session storage (a script reading the pane's DOM; `stable_for: 0.5`), with a message saying the saved sign-in did not appear in the Site data tab.
